The\n14\t * license key IS the wallet, so this is the only way to obtain a key: a guest\n15\t * can buy credits with no key and the webhook mints + emails one. Topping up an\n16\t * existing key is the other path.\n17\t *\n18\t * REQUEST BODY:\n19\t * - amount: whole dollars, 5..500 (1000 credits per $1)\n20\t * - licenseKey (optional): when present, top up that key instead of minting\n21\t * - email (optional): mint path only. Prefills the Stripe checkout email so the\n22\t *   minted key is emailed to that address; ignored when topping up a key.\n23\t *\n24\t * PRICING (two non-bundled line items):\n25\t * - Credits: amount * 100 cents -> amount * 1000 credits\n26\t * - Processing fee (6%): round(amount * 100 * 0.06) cents — revenue, never\n27\t *   granted as credits, and non-refundable.\n28\t *\n29\t * STRIPE METADATA (read by the webhook):\n30\t * - purchase_type: \"credits\"\n31\t * - credit_amount: credits to grant\n32\t * - fee_cents: the processing fee charged\n33\t * - license_key: present ONLY when topping up an existing key (absent => mint)\n34\t */\n35\t\n36\texport async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n37\t  try {\n38\t    const body = await req.json();\n39\t    const { licenseKey, amount, email } = body as {\n40\t      licenseKey?: unknown;\n41\t      amount?: unknown;\n42\t      email?: unknown;\n43\t    };\n44\t\n45\t    // Validate amount: whole dollars within [MIN, MAX].\n46\t    const amountError = validateCreditPurchaseAmount(amount);\n47\t    if (amountError !== null) {\n48\t      return NextResponse.json({ error: amountError }, { status: 400 });\n49\t    }\n50\t\n51\t    const { creditAmount, creditCents, feeCents } = computeCreditPurchase(\n52\t      amount as number\n53\t    );\n54\t\n55\t    const siteUrl =\n56\t      process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL || \"https://hyperwhisper.com\";\n57\t\n58\t    // Optional license key: when supplied, this is a top-up of an existing\n59\t    // wallet; when absent, the webhook mints a brand-new key on payment.\n60\t    // Trim first: a whitespace-only key must NOT count as \"present\" (which would\n61\t    // skip the mint path and then fail the key lookup). Normalize once and use the\n62\t    // trimmed value everywhere below.\n63\t    const normalizedLicenseKey =\n64\t      typeof licenseKey === \"string\" && licenseKey.trim().length > 0\n65\t        ? licenseKey.trim()\n66\t        : undefined;\n67\t    const hasLicenseKey = normalizedLicenseKey !== undefined;\n68\t\n69\t    // Mint path only: a guest-supplied email to prefill at checkout. Loose\n70\t    // format check — Stripe re-validates and the buyer can still edit it. The\n71\t    // webhook mints/emails the key off the email Stripe ultimately collects.\n72\t    const guestEmail =\n73\t      !hasLicenseKey &&\n74\t      typeof email === \"string\" &&\n75\t      /^[^\\s@]+@[^\\s@]+\\.[^\\s@]+$/.test(email.trim())\n76\t        ? email.trim().toLowerCase()\n77\t        : undefined;\n78\t\n79\t    const metadata: Record<string, string> = {\n80\t      purchase_type: \"credits\",\n81\t      credit_amount: creditAmount.toString(),\n82\t      fee_cents: feeCents.toString(),\n83\t    };\n84\t\n85\t    // Resolve the Stripe customer for the top-up path so credits attach to the\n86\t    // same customer/email. The mint path lets Stripe collect the email itself.\n87\t    let stripeCustomerId: string | undefined;\n88\t\n89\t    if (normalizedLicenseKey) {\n90\t      const license = await findAccountByKey(normalizedLicenseKey);\n91\t\n92\t      if (!license) {\n93\t        console.error(\n94\t          \"License lookup failed for key:\",\n95\t          normalizedLicenseKey.substring(0, 7)\n96\t        );\n97\t        return NextResponse.json(\n98\t          { error: \"Invalid license key\" },\n99\t          { status: 400 }\n100\t        );\n101\t      }\n102\t\n103\t      // Reject non-granted (e.g. revoked) licenses: credits added to them would\n104\t      // be unusable because /api/license/credits refuses to validate or deduct\n105\t      // them.\n106\t      if (license.status !== \"granted\") {\n107\t        return NextResponse.json(\n108\t          { error: `License is ${license.status}` },\n109\t          { status: 400 }\n110\t        );\n111\t      }\n112\t\n113\t      const email = license.email;\n114\t      if (!email) {\n115\t        return NextResponse.json(\n116\t          { error: \"License has no email associated\" },\n117\t          { status: 400 }\n118\t        );\n119\t      }\n120\t\n121\t      metadata.license_key = normalizedLicenseKey;\n122\t\n123\t      // Find or create the Stripe customer by email and cache it on the license.\n124\t      stripeCustomerId = license.stripeCustomerId ?? undefined;\n125\t      if (!stripeCustomerId) {\n126\t        const existingCustomers = await stripe.customers.list({\n127\t          email,\n128\t          limit: 1,\n129\t        });\n130\t\n131\t        if (existingCustomers.data.length > 0) {\n132\t          stripeCustomerId = existingCustomers.data[0].id;\n133\t        } else {\n134\t          const newCustomer = await stripe.customers.create({\n135\t            email,\n136\t            metadata: { license_key: normalizedLicenseKey },\n137\t          });\n138\t          stripeCustomerId = newCustomer.id;\n139\t        }\n140\t\n141\t        await updateAccountKey(license.id, { stripeCustomerId });\n142\t      }\n143\t    }\n144\t\n145\t    // Create Stripe Checkout Session\n146\t    // @ts-expect-error - managed_payments is in private preview\n147\t    const session = await stripe.checkout.sessions.create({\n148\t      mode: \"payment\",\n149\t      // Top-up: attach to the resolved customer. Mint: always create a customer\n150\t      // during checkout, prefilling the guest email when one was supplied.\n151\t      ...(stripeCustomerId\n152\t        ? { customer: stripeCustomerId }\n153\t        : {\n154\t            customer_creation: \"always\",\n155\t            ...(guestEmail ? { customer_email: guestEmail } : {}),\n156\t          }),\n157\t      line_items: [\n158\t        {\n159\t          price_data: {\n160\t            currency: \"usd\",\n161\t            product_data: {\n162\t              name: \"HyperWhisper Cloud credits\",\n163\t              description: `${creditAmount.toLocaleString()} credits`,\n164\t              // Managed Payments requires every line item's product to carry a\n165\t              // tax_code. txcd_10000000 = \"General - Electronically Supplied\n166\t              // Services\", which fits prepaid credits for a cloud STT service.\n167\t              tax_code: \"txcd_10000000\",\n168\t            },\n169\t            unit_amount: creditCents,\n170\t          },\n171\t          quantity: 1,\n172\t        },\n173\t        {\n174\t          price_data: {\n175\t            currency: \"usd\",\n176\t            product_data: {\n177\t              name: \"Cloud processing fee (6%)\",\n178\t              description: \"Non-refundable HyperWhisper Cloud processing fees\",\n179\t              // Same Managed Payments requirement: the fee line needs a tax_code.\n180\t              tax_code: \"txcd_10000000\",\n181\t            },\n182\t            unit_amount: feeCents,\n183\t          },\n184\t          quantity: 1,\n185\t        },\n186\t      ],\n187\t\n188\t      // Allow coupon/promotion codes\n189\t      allow_promotion_codes: true,\n190\t\n191\t      // Metadata for webhook processing\n192\t      metadata,\n193\t\n194\t      // Managed Payments: Stripe handles tax, invoicing, and compliance\n195\t      managed_payments: { enabled: true },\n196\t\n197\t      // Land on the public confirmation page (works for guests, who have no\n198\t      // account yet). It only personalizes copy from `credits` — entitlement is\n199\t      // always enforced server-side via the webhook-minted key.\n200\t      success_url: `${siteUrl}/purchase-success?session_id={CHECKOUT_SESSION_ID}&credits=${creditAmount}`,\n201\t      cancel_url: `${siteUrl}/credits`,\n202\t    });\n203\t\n204\t    // Return checkout URL to client\n205\t    if (session.url) {\n206\t      return NextResponse.json({\n207\t        checkoutUrl: session.url,\n208\t      });\n209\t    } else {\n210\t      throw new Error(\"No checkout URL returned from Stripe\");\n211\t    }\n212\t  } catch (error) {\n213\t    console.error(\"Credit checkout error:\", error);\n214\t\n215\t    return NextResponse.json(\n216\t      {\n217\t        error: \"Failed to create checkout session\",\n218\t        details: error instanceof Error ? error.message : \"Unknown error\",\n219\t      },\n220\t      { status: 500 }\n221\t    );\n222\t  }\n223\t}\n224\t"}]},"uuid":"c11f22dc-29e6-4ff6-bbcd-7d9ed63a98b2","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:47:23.404Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/nextjs/app/api/checkout/credits/route.ts","content":"import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from \"next/server\";\n\nimport { stripe } from \"@/lib/clients/stripe\";\nimport { findAccountByKey, updateAccountKey } from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\nimport {\n  validateCreditPurchaseAmount,\n  computeCreditPurchase,\n} from \"./validation\";\n\n/**\n * Stripe Checkout API Route for Credit Purchases\n *\n * Creates a Stripe Checkout Session for HyperWhisper credit purchases. The\n * license key IS the wallet, so this is the only way to obtain a key: a guest\n * can buy credits with no key and the webhook mints + emails one. Topping up an\n * existing key is the other path.\n *\n * REQUEST BODY:\n * - amount: whole dollars, 5..500 (1000 credits per $1)\n * - licenseKey (optional): when present, top up that key instead of minting\n * - email (optional): mint path only. Prefills the Stripe checkout email so the\n *   minted key is emailed to that address; ignored when topping up a key.\n *\n * PRICING (two non-bundled line items):\n * - Credits: amount * 100 cents -> amount * 1000 credits\n * - Processing fee (6%): round(amount * 100 * 0.06) cents — revenue, never\n *   granted as credits, and non-refundable.\n *\n * STRIPE METADATA (read by the webhook):\n * - purchase_type: \"credits\"\n * - credit_amount: credits to grant\n * - fee_cents: the processing fee charged\n * - license_key: present ONLY when topping up an existing key (absent => mint)\n */\n\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n  try {\n    const body = await req.json();\n    const { licenseKey, amount, email } = body as {\n      licenseKey?: unknown;\n      amount?: unknown;\n      email?: unknown;\n    };\n\n    // Validate amount: whole dollars within [MIN, MAX].\n    const amountError = validateCreditPurchaseAmount(amount);\n    if (amountError !== null) {\n      return NextResponse.json({ error: amountError }, { status: 400 });\n    }\n\n    const { creditAmount, creditCents, feeCents } = computeCreditPurchase(\n      amount as number\n    );\n\n    const siteUrl =\n      process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL || \"https://hyperwhisper.com\";\n\n    // Optional license key: when supplied, this is a top-up of an existing\n    // wallet; when absent, the webhook mints a brand-new key on payment.\n    // Trim first: a whitespace-only key must NOT count as \"present\" (which would\n    // skip the mint path and then fail the key lookup). Normalize once and use the\n    // trimmed value everywhere below.\n    const normalizedLicenseKey =\n      typeof licenseKey === \"string\" && licenseKey.trim().length > 0\n        ? licenseKey.trim()\n        : undefined;\n    const hasLicenseKey = normalizedLicenseKey !== undefined;\n\n    // Mint path only: a guest-supplied email to prefill at checkout. Loose\n    // format check — Stripe re-validates and the buyer can still edit it. The\n    // webhook mints/emails the key off the email Stripe ultimately collects.\n    const guestEmail =\n      !hasLicenseKey &&\n      typeof email === \"string\" &&\n      /^[^\\s@]+@[^\\s@]+\\.[^\\s@]+$/.test(email.trim())\n        ? email.trim().toLowerCase()\n        : undefined;\n\n    const metadata: Record<string, string> = {\n      purchase_type: \"credits\",\n      credit_amount: creditAmount.toString(),\n      fee_cents: feeCents.toString(),\n    };\n\n    // Resolve the Stripe customer for the top-up path so credits attach to the\n    // same customer/email. The mint path lets Stripe collect the email itself.\n    let stripeCustomerId: string | undefined;\n\n    if (normalizedLicenseKey) {\n      const license = await findAccountByKey(normalizedLicenseKey);\n\n      if (!license) {\n        console.error(\n          \"License lookup failed for key:\",\n          normalizedLicenseKey.substring(0, 7)\n        );\n        return NextResponse.json(\n          { error: \"Invalid license key\" },\n          { status: 400 }\n        );\n      }\n\n      // Reject non-granted (e.g. revoked) licenses: credits added to them would\n      // be unusable because /api/license/credits refuses to validate or deduct\n      // them.\n      if (license.status !== \"granted\") {\n        return NextResponse.json(\n          { error: `License is ${license.status}` },\n          { status: 400 }\n        );\n      }\n\n      const email = license.email;\n      if (!email) {\n        return NextResponse.json(\n          { error: \"License has no email associated\" },\n          { status: 400 }\n        );\n      }\n\n      metadata.license_key = normalizedLicenseKey;\n\n      // Find or create the Stripe customer by email and cache it on the license.\n      stripeCustomerId = license.stripeCustomerId ?? undefined;\n      if (!stripeCustomerId) {\n        const existingCustomers = await stripe.customers.list({\n          email,\n          limit: 1,\n        });\n\n        if (existingCustomers.data.length > 0) {\n          stripeCustomerId = existingCustomers.data[0].id;\n        } else {\n          const newCustomer = await stripe.customers.create({\n            email,\n            metadata: { license_key: normalizedLicenseKey },\n          });\n          stripeCustomerId = newCustomer.id;\n        }\n\n        await updateAccountKey(license.id, { stripeCustomerId });\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Create Stripe Checkout Session\n    // @ts-expect-error - managed_payments is in private preview\n    const session = await stripe.checkout.sessions.create({\n      mode: \"payment\",\n      // Top-up: attach to the resolved customer. Mint: always create a customer\n      // during checkout, prefilling the guest email when one was supplied.\n      ...(stripeCustomerId\n        ? { customer: stripeCustomerId }\n        : {\n            customer_creation: \"always\",\n            ...(guestEmail ? { customer_email: guestEmail } : {}),\n          }),\n      line_items: [\n        {\n          price_data: {\n            currency: \"usd\",\n            product_data: {\n              name: \"HyperWhisper Cloud credits\",\n              description: `${creditAmount.toLocaleString()} credits`,\n              // Managed Payments requires every line item's product to carry a\n              // tax_code. txcd_10000000 = \"General - Electronically Supplied\n              // Services\", which fits prepaid credits for a cloud STT service.\n              tax_code: \"txcd_10000000\",\n            },\n            unit_amount: creditCents,\n          },\n          quantity: 1,\n        },\n        {\n          price_data: {\n            currency: \"usd\",\n            product_data: {\n              name: \"Cloud processing fee (6%)\",\n              description: \"Non-refundable HyperWhisper Cloud processing fees\",\n              // Same Managed Payments requirement: the fee line needs a tax_code.\n              tax_code: \"txcd_10000000\",\n            },\n            unit_amount: feeCents,\n          },\n          quantity: 1,\n        },\n      ],\n\n      // Allow coupon/promotion codes\n      allow_promotion_codes: true,\n\n      // Metadata for webhook processing\n      metadata,\n\n      // Managed Payments: Stripe handles tax, invoicing, and compliance\n      managed_payments: { enabled: true },\n\n      // Land on the public confirmation page (works for guests, who have no\n      // account yet). It only personalizes copy from `credits` — entitlement is\n      // always enforced server-side via the webhook-minted key.\n      success_url: `${siteUrl}/purchase-success?session_id={CHECKOUT_SESSION_ID}&credits=${creditAmount}`,\n      cancel_url: `${siteUrl}/credits`,\n    });\n\n    // Return checkout URL to client\n    if (session.url) {\n      return NextResponse.json({\n        checkoutUrl: session.url,\n      });\n    } else {\n      throw new Error(\"No checkout URL returned from Stripe\");\n    }\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error(\"Credit checkout error:\", error);\n\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      {\n        error: \"Failed to create checkout session\",\n        details: error instanceof Error ? error.message : \"Unknown error\",\n      },\n      { status: 500 }\n    );\n  }\n}\n","numLines":224,"startLine":1,"totalLines":224}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"960cb0c2-5ce5-4420-a69b-b25ae93cec80","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{\n  index,\n  numeric,\n  pgTable,\n  text,\n  timestamp,\n  uniqueIndex,\n  uuid,\n} from \"drizzle-orm/pg-core\";\nimport { relations } from \"drizzle-orm\";\nimport { user } from .references(() => user.id, { onDelete: \"cascade\" }),\n    sourceType: text(\"source_type\").notNull(),\n    sourceId: text(\"source_id\").notNull(),\n    originalAmount: numeric(\"original_amount\", {"parentUuid":"16f6ffea-2c49-4e40-9d41-00233d97491b","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ac25c34de25263649","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdWJ4LpDhCHD9KEqtQKL9","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01A4UDFqYY4BrtmScgZwsZnm","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-ray-Desktop-ray-os/0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c/scratchpad/map.md"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":5051,"cache_read_input_tokens":53729,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":5051,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdWJ4HoPqCTehMwQCJtSb","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"4d3b03f3-8440-4b6b-8285-ae29d09fe7c1","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:47:31.126Z","effort":"high","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}
{"parentUuid":"4d3b03f3-8440-4b6b-8285-ae29d09fe7c1","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"872714b7-1488-4cc4-8bad-5341f8c86fd3","agentId":"ac25c34de25263649","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01A4UDFqYY4BrtmScgZwsZnm","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t## Stack & surfaces\n2\t\n3\tTwo independently deployed TypeScript services. **`nextjs/`** is a Next.js 15.5 App Router app on Vercel (React 18, next-intl v4 with 40 locales, HeroUI/Tailwind, Drizzle ORM over `node-postgres`, Better Auth with a magic-link plugin plus a custom `license-key` sign-in plugin, tRPC v10 with superjson, Stripe v20 + a legacy Polar SDK path, Resend for email, Upstash Redis for rate limiting). It is the **system of record for money**: license keys (\"Account Keys\"), the `credit_grants` ledger, the `credit_balances` denormalized cache, and `stripe_processed_events` idempotency table all live here, exposed to the outside world through 23 REST route handlers plus one tRPC catch-all. **`hyperwhisper-cloud/`** is a Bun/Hono service on Fly.io that proxies audio to 11 STT providers and 7 LLM providers; it holds **no database** — it authenticates by POSTing the license key to `nextjs`'s `/api/license/validate`, caches the `{isValid, credits}` verdict in Upstash Redis for 1h (`license:<key>`), and charges usage by POSTing back to `/api/license/credits`. Trust boundaries: the Fly service trusts only the TCP peer address (`fdaa::/16` ⇒ off-edge ⇒ IP is `unknown`); the Next.js service trusts the Stripe signature, an `x-internal-secret` header, a Better Auth session cookie, and — critically — **the bare license key as a bearer credential** for all `/api/license/*` and `/api/account/*` endpoints. Shared mutable state: `inFlightDeductions: Set<Promise>` (cloud, drained on SIGTERM), the `_jwtClient`/`_inflight` Google token singletons, the ~1h in-memory model-list cache in `nextjs/lib/services/model-list.ts`, and the Redis license cache which both services write. Concurrency points: fire-and-forget `deductCredits()` after response flush, `costPromise.then(...)` after an SSE stream, the Deepgram WebSocket bridge, Stripe webhook re-deliveries, and `db.transaction` blocks in `db-layer.ts`.\n4\t\n5\t## Entry points\n6\t\n7\t**hyperwhisper-cloud (Hono, `src/index.ts`)**\n8\t- `GET /health`, `GET /warmup` — unauthenticated, no input.\n9\t- `POST /transcribe` (`src/routes/transcribe.ts`) — raw `audio/*` body; `X-STT-Provider`/`X-STT-Model`/`X-STT-Domain` headers; `account_key`|`license_key`, `language`, `initial_prompt`, `mode` query params read via `rawQuery()`. Buffers up to 2 GB into RAM.\n10\t- `POST /post-process` (`src/routes/post-process.ts`) — JSON `{text ≤100k chars, prompt, account_key|license_key}`; `X-LLM-Provider`/model headers.\n11\t- `POST /assistant` (`src/routes/assistant.ts`) — `multipart/form-data`: `account_key`, `messages` (JSON, ≤6 MB, may contain inline base64 images), `prompt`, `image` file (≤10 MB). Returns an SSE stream from Anthropic.\n12\t- `GET /usage` (`src/routes/usage.ts`) — `account_key`|`license_key`|`identifier`, `force_refresh` query params.\n13\t- `GET /ws/streaming-deepgram` (`src/routes/ws-streaming-deepgram.ts`) — WebSocket upgrade; `account_key`, `language`, `vocabulary` query params; binary audio frames proxied to `wss://api.deepgram.com`.\n14\t- `SIGTERM`/`SIGINT` handler — drains `inFlightDeductions` for 4 s.\n15\t- `hyperwhisper-cloud/scripts/smoke-test.ts` — operator-run, hits the live service.\n16\t\n17\t**nextjs — public REST (all unauthenticated unless noted)**\n18\t- `POST /api/license/validate` + alias `/api/account/validate` — `{license_key, include_credits, device_id, device_name}`. IP rate-limited 30/min. Falls back to a live Polar API call and can **mint a license + 5000 credits** on a DB miss.\n19\t- `POST /api/license/activate` + `/api/account/activate` — same shape, rate-limited, legacy.\n20\t- `POST /api/license/deactivate` + `/api/account/deactivate` — stub, always 200.\n21\t- `GET /api/license/credits` + `/api/account/credits` — `?license_key=`; returns balance. **No rate limit.**\n22\t- `POST /api/license/credits` + `/api/account/credits` — `{license_key, amount, metadata}`; **atomically deducts `amount` credits. No rate limit, no auth beyond the key.** This is the sole write path used by the Fly service.\n23\t- `POST /api/checkout/credits` — `{amount: 5..500, licenseKey?, email?}`; creates Stripe customers and Checkout Sessions. **No auth, no rate limit.**\n24\t- `GET /api/checkout` — redirect to `/credits`.\n25\t- `POST /api/webhooks/stripe` — Stripe signature verified; handles `checkout.session.completed`, `checkout.session.async_payment_succeeded`, `checkout.session.async_payment_failed`, `charge.refunded`.\n26\t- `POST /api/webhooks/add-blog-post` — Outrank bearer token (+ optional HMAC); upserts `blog_posts` and calls `revalidatePath`.\n27\t- `GET /api/download` — `?platform=&arch=`; fetches an appcast XML and builds a dynamic `RegExp` from `arch`.\n28\t- `GET /api/config`, `GET /api/internal/models`, `GET /models` — cached public JSON; `/models` and `/api/internal/models` fan out to 6 paid provider list APIs on a cache miss.\n29\t- `GET|POST /api/auth/[...all]` — Better Auth (magic link, `/sign-in/license-key` at 5/min).\n30\t- `GET|POST /api/trpc/[trpc]` — `download.*` (public), `customer.*` (protected), `admin.*` (admin).\n31\t- `GET /api/customer/profile` — Better Auth session required.\n32\t- `GET /[locale]/user/auth/sign-out` — route handler.\n33\t\n34\t**nextjs — internal REST (`x-internal-secret` header, timing-safe)**\n35\t- `POST /api/internal/grant-license` — mints a key + credits for an email.\n36\t- `POST /api/internal/granted-emails` — bulk email dump.\n37\t- `POST /api/internal/licenses-for-email` — keys + balances for an email.\n38\t\n39\t**nextjs — middleware & rendering**\n40\t- `middleware.ts` — matcher `/((?!api|models(?:/|$)|_next|_vercel|docs|.*\\..*).*)`. Gates `/{locale}/user/customers` with a real `fetch('/api/auth/get-session')` + `role === \"admin\"`; gates all other `/{locale}/user/*` on **cookie presence only**; runs `next-intl` for everything else.\n41\t- 22 `page.tsx` files under `app/[locale]/`, plus `sitemap.ts` / `robots.ts`.\n42\t\n43\t## Invariants (ranked)\n44\t\n45\t### I1: Every credit reservation taken at preflight is ≥ the total cost actually deducted for that request, across all retries, fallbacks and re-prompts.\n46\tWhy it matters: the reservation is the only thing standing between a low-balance user and unbounded provider spend the business eats; under-reserving turns each request into a free-money bug at HyperWhisper's expense.\n47\tSearch strategy: `grep -n \"ESTIMATED_POST_PROCESS_CREDITS\\|ESTIMATED_ASSISTANT_CREDITS\\|estimateCreditsForProviderFallbacks\\|minimumStreamingCredits\"` — then trace each. `post-process.ts` reserves a **flat `1.0`** yet can spend `retriesFor(provider)` primary attempts (up to 3 for groq) + a fallback provider's full retry budget + a *prompt-leakage retry on a third provider* whose cost is `costUsd += retryResponse.costUsd`. `assistant.ts` reserves `3.0 × countInlineImages()` but the multipart `image` file adds an image `countInlineImages` never counts, and output tokens are unbounded. In `transcribe.ts`, cross-check `FALLBACK_CHAINS` against the `rates` array built in `estimateCreditsForProviderFallbacks` — grep `costUsd +=`, `retriesFor(`, `callWithRetry(`.\n48\t\n49\t### I2: Every upstream call that has already cost money results in exactly one `deductCredits()`, on every exit path — including thrown-exception and stream-error paths — and never more than one.\n50\tWhy it matters: a missed deduction is silent revenue loss at scale; a duplicated one double-charges a paying customer.\n51\tSearch strategy: in `hyperwhisper-cloud/src/routes/`, grep `deductCredits(` (7 call sites) and `return c.json\\|return errorResponse\\|return new Response`; for each `return` that follows a successful provider call, confirm a deduction precedes it. Specific suspects: `assistant.ts:350` — `costPromise.then(...)` bills only on resolve, so a rejected `costPromise` (Anthropic stream aborted mid-flight after tokens were billed) drops the charge entirely; `ws-streaming-deepgram.ts` `endSession()` is guarded by `sessionEnded` but is reachable from `onClose`, `onError`, and the Deepgram `close` listener concurrently. Also grep `performDeduction` — any call site bypassing `deductCredits()` escapes `inFlightDeductions` and is lost on SIGTERM.\n52\t\n53\t### I3: A `license:<key>` Redis entry with `isValid:false` is written only for a *definitive* verdict (HTTP 4xx from the licensing API); transient failures (5xx, timeout, network, unparseable body) must leave the cache untouched.\n54\tWhy it matters: `LICENSE_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS = 3600` — one Vercel cold start or a malformed body during a deploy locks a paying customer out of transcription for a full hour across every Fly region.\n55\tSearch strategy: `grep -rn \"cacheLicense(\" hyperwhisper-cloud/src`. `middleware/auth.ts:70-79` implements the rule correctly (explicit `if (response.status >= 500) return ... ; // not caching`). `routes/usage.ts:44-52` (`validateLicenseAndGetCredits`) does **not**: it computes `isValid = data.valid === true` after `response.json().catch(() => ({}))` and unconditionally calls `cacheLicense` — so a 500, an HTML error page, or an empty body all poison the cache with `isValid:false`. Verify every `cacheLicense` call is preceded by a status check.\n56\t\n57\t### I4: Every STT adapter that returns a non-empty transcript returns a strictly positive `durationSeconds` (falling back to `estimateSecondsFromBytes` when the upstream duration field is absent or zero).\n58\tWhy it matters: `costUsd` is `durationSeconds × rate` for duration-billed providers, so a missing field means a real, provider-billed transcription is charged **0 credits** — a free-transcription oracle triggered by any upstream response-shape change.\n59\tSearch strategy: `grep -ln \"estimateSecondsFromBytes\" hyperwhisper-cloud/src/providers/*.ts` and diff against the 11 STT adapters. Present in `assemblyai.ts`, `mistral.ts`, `soniox.ts`, `openai.ts`, and `google-chirp.ts` (via its own `estimateAudioSeconds`); **absent in `deepgram.ts`, `groq.ts`, `elevenlabs.ts`, `xai-stt.ts`, `azure-mai.ts`**. For each, read where `durationSeconds` is derived (`data.metadata.duration`, `data.duration`, last word `.end`, `data.durationMilliseconds`) and confirm a guard before `compute*TranscriptionCost(...)`.\n60\t\n61\t### I5: Every Stripe webhook handler that mutates licenses or credits dedupes on a stable Stripe id before mutating, and returns non-2xx (so Stripe retries) on any *transient* failure.\n62\tWhy it matters: Stripe delivers at-least-once and fires both `checkout.session.completed` and `checkout.session.async_payment_succeeded` for the same purchase; a missing guard double-grants credits, and swallowing an error returns 200 and permanently loses the grant.\n63\tSearch strategy: in `nextjs/lib/services/stripe-webhook.ts`, grep `grantCreditLot(\\|grantCreditsForStripeEvent(\\|refundCreditGrant(\\|updateAccountKey(`. `handleCreditPurchase` correctly routes through `grantCreditsForStripeEvent` (keyed on `stripe_processed_events.stripe_object_id`); `handleLicensePurchase:126` calls the **un-deduped `grantCreditLot({sourceType:\"license_bundle\", sourceId: session.id})`** and its only guard is a prior `findAccountByStripeSession` read — check whether `grantCreditLotInTransaction`'s `onConflictDoNothing` target actually covers `(sourceType, sourceId)`. Also `app/api/webhooks/stripe/route.ts:121-130`: `handleChargeRefunded` errors are caught and the route still returns `{received:true}` — a refund clawback lost to a transient DB blip is never retried.\n64\t\n65\t### I6: No credit amount, price, or quantity that determines what a user is charged or granted is taken from client-controlled input without server-side re-derivation or a hard bound.\n66\tWhy it matters: this is the direct path to minting free credits or draining another key's wallet.\n67\tSearch strategy: `grep -rn \"body.amount\\|input.amount\\|metadata.credit_amount\\|creditAmount\" nextjs/app/api nextjs/lib nextjs/server`. `POST /api/license/credits` accepts `amount` straight off the wire (bounded only by `validateCreditDeductionAmount`: finite, >0, ≤1,000,000, 2 dp) and deducts it — with the license key as the sole credential and no rate limit, so anyone holding a leaked key can zero a balance in one request. `session.metadata.credit_amount` is trusted in `handleCreditPurchase`; confirm nothing but `/api/checkout/credits` can write that metadata. Confirm `computeCreditPurchase()` is the only source of `creditCents`/`feeCents` and that `components/credits/CreditsPurchase.tsx:92` sends only `amount`, never a price.\n68\t\n69\t### I7: Every unauthenticated Next.js route handler whose work fans out to a paid third party or writes a durable object is rate-limited.\n70\tWhy it matters: one cheap POST maps 1:1 to a Polar call, a Stripe customer/session creation, or a 6-provider model fan-out — free amplification and quota/cost burn against an open endpoint.\n71\tSearch strategy: `grep -rLn \"RateLimiter\\|\\.limit(\" nextjs/app/api/**/route.ts` and compare against `nextjs/lib/rate-limit.ts` (only `downloadEmailRateLimiter` and `licenseValidateRateLimiter` exist). `/api/license/validate` and `/api/license/activate` are covered; `/api/license/credits` (GET **and** the deducting POST), `/api/checkout/credits` (creates real `stripe.customers` + Checkout Sessions), `/api/download` (outbound appcast fetch + dynamic RegExp), and `/api/internal/models` are not. For `/api/internal/models` and `/models`, verify the `s-maxage=3600` header plus `fetchAvailableModels`'s in-memory TTL actually bound the fan-out on a cache-busted or cold-lambda request.\n72\t\n73\t### I8: Any handler returning or mutating a specific user's data derives that user from the Better Auth session (or an admin check), never from a client-supplied id; middleware cookie-presence is never the only gate.\n74\tWhy it matters: `middleware.ts` checks only that `better-auth.session_token` **exists** for `/{locale}/user/*` — a forged or expired cookie value passes it. If a page or procedure then trusts that, an unauthenticated caller reads customer data.\n75\tSearch strategy: for each of `app/[locale]/user/(authenticated)/{dashboard,devices,customers}/page.tsx` and `layout.tsx`, grep for `auth.api.getSession`; any page that renders account data without one relies on the presence-only check. Cross-check the tRPC routers: `grep -n \"publicProcedure\\|protectedProcedure\\|adminProcedure\" nextjs/server/api/routers/**/*.ts` — every `admin/*` procedure must use `adminProcedure` (`ctx.isAdmin` from `user.role === \"admin\"`), and every `customer.*` procedure must key off `ctx.user.id`/`ctx.user.email`, never an `input`-supplied id. Also verify `src/db/schema/auth.ts` marks `role` as `input: false` so it cannot be set at sign-up.\n76\t\n77\t### I9: Every comparison of a caller-supplied credential against an env secret is timing-safe **and** fails closed when the env var is unset or empty.\n78\tWhy it matters: `timingSafeEqualSecret(received, expected)` returns `false` when `expected` is falsy — good — but any `===` comparison, or a route that skips the check when the env var is missing, silently opens an admin/mint endpoint in a misconfigured environment.\n79\tSearch strategy: `grep -rn \"process.env\\..*SECRET\\|process.env\\..*TOKEN\\|process.env\\..*KEY\" nextjs/app/api nextjs/lib | grep -n \"===\\|!==\\|==\"`. Confirm all three `/api/internal/*` routes call `timingSafeEqualSecret(secret, process.env.HYPERWHISPER_INTERNAL_SECRET)` before any DB work (they do at line ~11 of each). In `app/api/webhooks/add-blog-post/route.ts`, check the interaction between `OUTRANK_WEBHOOK_SIGNING_SECRET` and `OUTRANK_WEBHOOK_TOKEN`: when signing is enabled, verify the bearer path cannot be used as an alternative bypass, and that a missing `OUTRANK_WEBHOOK_TOKEN` doesn't make `timingSafeEqualSecret` the only barrier to a mint-shaped mutation. Also `app/api/webhooks/stripe/route.ts:33-40` correctly 500s on a missing `STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET` — confirm no sibling handler does the opposite.\n80\t\n81\t### I10: Every `fetch()` to a third-party API carries an `AbortSignal.timeout` or an `AbortController`.\n82\tWhy it matters: a hung upstream leaves an unresolved promise pinning the request scope — on a 1 GB Fly machine that bloats the Bun worker until OOM-kill, and on Vercel it burns the full function max-duration.\n83\tSearch strategy: `grep -rn \"fetch(\" hyperwhisper-cloud/src nextjs/lib nextjs/app | grep -v \"signal:\"`. In the cloud service all 10 STT adapters route through `fetchWithTimeout()` in `providers/utils.ts` (default `DEFAULT_PROVIDER_TIMEOUT_MS = 15_000`), but **all 7 LLM provider files — `anthropic.ts`, `cerebras.ts`, `groq-llm.ts`, `gemini-llm.ts`, `mistral-llm.ts`, `openai-llm.ts`, `xai-llm.ts` — use bare `fetch()` with no timeout** (`streamAnthropicChat`'s AbortController fires only on client disconnect, not on a stall). Note `gcs-storage.ts` maintains a *second, duplicated* `fetchWithTimeoutMs` implementation that can drift from `providers/utils.ts`. On the Next.js side check `lib/services/model-list.ts` `apiGet()` and the Polar SDK calls in `src/lib/license-validation.ts` (no timeout is configurable there, yet it sits behind an unauthenticated endpoint).\n84\t\n85\t### I11: In the WebSocket bridge, every acquired resource is released on every path, and closing one side always closes the other.\n86\tWhy it matters: an orphaned upstream Deepgram socket keeps metering billable audio and holds a Fly file descriptor; a leaked `setInterval` survives the request.\n87\tSearch strategy: read `hyperwhisper-cloud/src/routes/ws-streaming-deepgram.ts` end to end and check each early `return`. The `MAX_SESSION_AUDIO_BYTES` breach at line 308 sends an error and calls `clientSocket.close(1009)` but **never calls `closeUpstream()` / `deepgramWs.close()`** — verify whether the resulting client close reliably fires `onClose`. Also: `pingInterval` is cleared only inside `endSession()`; the `apiKey`-missing early return at line 238 sets it never, but confirm no path sets the interval then exits without `endSession()`. Grep `setInterval(\\|clearInterval(\\|getReader()\\|releaseLock()\\|\\.cancel(` across `src/` — `providers/anthropic.ts`'s `streamAnthropicChat` `start()` catch block never calls `reader.cancel()` when the failure is not a client abort.\n88\t\n89\t### I12: The provider and model reported back to the client (`X-STT-Provider`, `X-STT-Model`, deduction `metadata`) always names the provider/model that actually ran and was priced.\n90\tWhy it matters: `transcribe.ts:669` does `result.source as Provider` and looks the price up from that; if an adapter returns a `source` that isn't its own registry id, or reports a `model` it didn't run, the charge is computed against the wrong rate table and support has no audit trail.\n91\tSearch strategy: `grep -n \"source:\" hyperwhisper-cloud/src/providers/*.ts` and check each returned literal against `TranscriptionSource` in `providers/types.ts` **and** against `SttProviderId` in `lib/stt-models.ts` — these are two parallel unions that must stay in sync (`TranscriptionSource` additionally carries `'no_speech'`). Then confirm `usedModel = result.model || attemptModel` (line 511) is what reaches both the `X-STT-Model` header and `stt_model` in the `deductCredits` metadata, and that `getProviderDef(current).defaultModel` on a fallback hop matches the rate actually used in `estimateCreditsForProviderFallbacks`. Also check `cost-calculator.ts`'s `rates[model] ?? rates[defaultModel]` fallback — an unknown model id is silently re-priced at the default rate.\n92\t\n93\t### I13: Every value crossing a trust boundary is validated (or allow-listed/escaped) before it is used to index, interpolate, allocate, or render.\n94\tWhy it matters: these are the crash-and-injection sinks — an unvalidated `req.json()` destructure 500s, an unescaped name lands script in a transactional email from a trusted sender, an unbounded `Content-Length` allocates memory before auth.\n95\tSearch strategy: (a) `grep -rn \"await req.json()\\|await request.json()\\|c.req.json()\" nextjs/app/api hyperwhisper-cloud/src` and confirm each result is shape-checked before destructuring — `app/api/license/credits/route.ts:82` does `const body = await req.json(); const { license_key, amount, metadata } = body;` with no null guard and an entirely unvalidated `metadata` that is then logged. (b) `grep -rn \"escapeHtml\" nextjs/lib/templates/` — all four templates escape `customerName`; verify every *other* interpolated field in the 17/15/21/14 `${...}` sites is either a number or system-generated. (c) `grep -rn \"dangerouslySetInnerHTML\" nextjs` — `app/[locale]/blog/[slug]/page.tsx:170` renders `post.html` raw from the Outrank webhook. (d) `grep -rn \"new RegExp(\" nextjs` — `app/api/download/route.ts:66` builds a RegExp from `arch`, guarded by `parseWindowsArch`'s allow-list; confirm no sibling does the same without one. (e) Confirm `Content-Length` is parsed and bounded *before* `arrayBuffer()`/`formData()` on every cloud route.\n96\t\n97\t### I14: No user-facing path hardcodes a single locale, and every message key referenced by a component exists in all 40 locale files.\n98\tWhy it matters: `localePrefix: \"always\"` over 40 locales means a hardcoded `/en/...` silently strips 39 languages from a cache invalidation or a post-auth redirect, and a missing key makes `next-intl` throw at render — a hard 500 on that locale only, invisible from an English-language smoke test.\n99\tSearch strategy: `grep -rn '\"/en/\\|`/en/\\|/en/' nextjs/app nextjs/src nextjs/lib` — `app/api/webhooks/add-blog-post/route.ts:386,403` calls `revalidatePath(\"/en/blog/...\")` and `revalidatePath(\"/en/blog\")` only, so 39 locales keep serving stale blog indexes; `src/lib/license-key-redirect.ts:1` pins `DEFAULT_LICENSE_KEY_REDIRECT = \"/en/user/dashboard\"`. Then diff key sets: `for f in nextjs/messages/*.json; do echo \"$f $(python3 -c 'import json,sys;d=json.load(open(sys.argv[1]));print(len(json.dumps(d)))' $f)\"; done` — better, flatten and set-diff each of the 40 files against `en.json`. Finally, `grep -rLn \"setRequestLocale\" nextjs/app/\\[locale\\]/**/page.tsx` — only `app/[locale]/layout.tsx` calls it today, so verify against `routing.ts` whether any statically-rendered page under `[locale]` needs it to avoid bailing to dynamic rendering or leaking the wrong locale.\n100\t\n101\t## Partition\n102\t\n103\t*(verified programmatically: all 210 files in `bug-hunt/scope-files.txt` land in exactly one bucket, no file matched two)*\n104\t\n105\t### B1: cloud-entry-and-auth — 10 files\n106\t- `hyperwhisper-cloud/src/index.ts`\n107\t- `hyperwhisper-cloud/src/middleware/` (auth.ts, Stack & surfaces\n\nTwo independently deployed TypeScript services. **`nextjs/`** is a Next.js 15.5 App Router app on Vercel (React 18, next-intl v4 with 40 locales, HeroUI/Tailwind, Drizzle ORM over `node-postgres`, Better Auth with a magic-link plugin plus a custom `license-key` sign-in plugin, tRPC v10 with superjson, Stripe v20 + a legacy Polar SDK path, Resend for email, Upstash Redis for rate limiting). It is the **system of record for money**: license keys (\"Account Keys\"), the `credit_grants` ledger, the `credit_balances` denormalized cache, and `stripe_processed_events` idempotency table all live here, exposed to the outside world through 23 REST route handlers plus one tRPC catch-all. **`hyperwhisper-cloud/`** is a Bun/Hono service on Fly.io that proxies audio to 11 STT providers and 7 LLM providers; it holds **no database** — it authenticates by POSTing the license key to `nextjs`'s `/api/license/validate`, caches the `{isValid, credits}` verdict in Upstash Redis for 1h (`license:<key>`), and charges usage by POSTing back to `/api/license/credits`. Trust boundaries: the Fly service trusts only the TCP peer address (`fdaa::/16` ⇒ off-edge ⇒ IP is `unknown`); the Next.js service trusts the Stripe signature, an `x-internal-secret` header, a Better Auth session cookie, and — critically — **the bare license key as a bearer credential** for all `/api/license/*` and `/api/account/*` endpoints. Shared mutable state: `inFlightDeductions: Set<Promise>` (cloud, drained on SIGTERM), the `_jwtClient`/`_inflight` Google token singletons, the ~1h in-memory model-list cache in `nextjs/lib/services/model-list.ts`, and the Redis license cache which both services write. Concurrency points: fire-and-forget `deductCredits()` after response flush, `costPromise.then(...)` after an SSE stream, the Deepgram WebSocket bridge, Stripe webhook re-deliveries, and `db.transaction` blocks in `db-layer.ts`.\n\n## Entry points\n\n**hyperwhisper-cloud (Hono, `src/index.ts`)**\n- `GET /health`, `GET /warmup` — unauthenticated, no input.\n- `POST /transcribe` (`src/routes/transcribe.ts`) — raw `audio/*` body; `X-STT-Provider`/`X-STT-Model`/`X-STT-Domain` headers; `account_key`|`license_key`, `language`, `initial_prompt`, `mode` query params read via `rawQuery()`. Buffers up to 2 GB into RAM.\n- `POST /post-process` (`src/routes/post-process.ts`) — JSON `{text ≤100k chars, prompt, account_key|license_key}`; `X-LLM-Provider`/model headers.\n- `POST /assistant` (`src/routes/assistant.ts`) — `multipart/form-data`: `account_key`, `messages` (JSON, ≤6 MB, may contain inline base64 images), `prompt`, `image` file (≤10 MB). Returns an SSE stream from Anthropic.\n- `GET /usage` (`src/routes/usage.ts`) — `account_key`|`license_key`|`identifier`, `force_refresh` query params.\n- `GET /ws/streaming-deepgram` (`src/routes/ws-streaming-deepgram.ts`) — WebSocket upgrade; `account_key`, `language`, `vocabulary` query params; binary audio frames proxied to `wss://api.deepgram.com`.\n- `SIGTERM`/`SIGINT` handler — drains `inFlightDeductions` for 4 s.\n- `hyperwhisper-cloud/scripts/smoke-test.ts` — operator-run, hits the live service.\n\n**nextjs — public REST (all unauthenticated unless noted)**\n- `POST /api/license/validate` + alias `/api/account/validate` — `{license_key, include_credits, device_id, device_name}`. IP rate-limited 30/min. Falls back to a live Polar API call and can **mint a license + 5000 credits** on a DB miss.\n- `POST /api/license/activate` + `/api/account/activate` — same shape, rate-limited, legacy.\n- `POST /api/license/deactivate` + `/api/account/deactivate` — stub, always 200.\n- `GET /api/license/credits` + `/api/account/credits` — `?license_key=`; returns balance. **No rate limit.**\n- `POST /api/license/credits` + `/api/account/credits` — `{license_key, amount, metadata}`; **atomically deducts `amount` credits. No rate limit, no auth beyond the key.** This is the sole write path used by the Fly service.\n- `POST /api/checkout/credits` — `{amount: 5..500, licenseKey?, email?}`; creates Stripe customers and Checkout Sessions. **No auth, no rate limit.**\n- `GET /api/checkout` — redirect to `/credits`.\n- `POST /api/webhooks/stripe` — Stripe signature verified; handles `checkout.session.completed`, `checkout.session.async_payment_succeeded`, `checkout.session.async_payment_failed`, `charge.refunded`.\n- `POST /api/webhooks/add-blog-post` — Outrank bearer token (+ optional HMAC); upserts `blog_posts` and calls `revalidatePath`.\n- `GET /api/download` — `?platform=&arch=`; fetches an appcast XML and builds a dynamic `RegExp` from `arch`.\n- `GET /api/config`, `GET /api/internal/models`, `GET /models` — cached public JSON; `/models` and `/api/internal/models` fan out to 6 paid provider list APIs on a cache miss.\n- `GET|POST /api/auth/[...all]` — Better Auth (magic link, `/sign-in/license-key` at 5/min).\n- `GET|POST /api/trpc/[trpc]` — `download.*` (public), `customer.*` (protected), `admin.*` (admin).\n- `GET /api/customer/profile` — Better Auth session required.\n- `GET /[locale]/user/auth/sign-out` — route handler.\n\n**nextjs — internal REST (`x-internal-secret` header, timing-safe)**\n- `POST /api/internal/grant-license` — mints a key + credits for an email.\n- `POST /api/internal/granted-emails` — bulk email dump.\n- `POST /api/internal/licenses-for-email` — keys + balances for an email.\n\n**nextjs — middleware & rendering**\n- `middleware.ts` — matcher `/((?!api|models(?:/|$)|_next|_vercel|docs|.*\\..*).*)`. Gates `/{locale}/user/customers` with a real `fetch('/api/auth/get-session')` + `role === \"admin\"`; gates all other `/{locale}/user/*` on **cookie presence only**; runs `next-intl` for everything else.\n- 22 `page.tsx` files under `app/[locale]/`, plus `sitemap.ts` / `robots.ts`.\n\n## Invariants (ranked)\n\n### I1: Every credit reservation taken at preflight is ≥ the total cost actually deducted for that request, across all retries, fallbacks and re-prompts.\nWhy it matters: the reservation is the only thing standing between a low-balance user and unbounded provider spend the business eats; under-reserving turns each request into a free-money bug at HyperWhisper's expense.\nSearch strategy: `grep -n \"ESTIMATED_POST_PROCESS_CREDITS\\|ESTIMATED_ASSISTANT_CREDITS\\|estimateCreditsForProviderFallbacks\\|minimumStreamingCredits\"` — then trace each. `post-process.ts` reserves a **flat `1.0`** yet can spend `retriesFor(provider)` primary attempts (up to 3 for groq) + a fallback provider's full retry budget + a *prompt-leakage retry on a third provider* whose cost is `costUsd += retryResponse.costUsd`. `assistant.ts` reserves `3.0 × countInlineImages()` but the multipart `image` file adds an image `countInlineImages` never counts, and output tokens are unbounded. In `transcribe.ts`, cross-check `FALLBACK_CHAINS` against the `rates` array built in `estimateCreditsForProviderFallbacks` — grep `costUsd +=`, `retriesFor(`, `callWithRetry(`.\n\n### I2: Every upstream call that has already cost money results in exactly one `deductCredits()`, on every exit path — including thrown-exception and stream-error paths — and never more than one.\nWhy it matters: a missed deduction is silent revenue loss at scale; a duplicated one double-charges a paying customer.\nSearch strategy: in `hyperwhisper-cloud/src/routes/`, grep `deductCredits(` (7 call sites) and `return c.json\\|return errorResponse\\|return new Response`; for each `return` that follows a successful provider call, confirm a deduction precedes it. Specific suspects: `assistant.ts:350` — `costPromise.then(...)` bills only on resolve, so a rejected `costPromise` (Anthropic stream aborted mid-flight after tokens were billed) drops the charge entirely; `ws-streaming-deepgram.ts` `endSession()` is guarded by `sessionEnded` but is reachable from `onClose`, `onError`, and the Deepgram `close` listener concurrently. Also grep `performDeduction` — any call site bypassing `deductCredits()` escapes `inFlightDeductions` and is lost on SIGTERM.\n\n### I3: A `license:<key>` Redis entry with `isValid:false` is written only for a *definitive* verdict (HTTP 4xx from the licensing API); transient failures (5xx, timeout, network, unparseable body) must leave the cache untouched.\nWhy it matters: `LICENSE_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS = 3600` — one Vercel cold start or a malformed body during a deploy locks a paying customer out of transcription for a full hour across every Fly region.\nSearch strategy: `grep -rn \"cacheLicense(\" hyperwhisper-cloud/src`. `middleware/auth.ts:70-79` implements the rule correctly (explicit `if (response.status >= 500) return ... ; // not caching`). `routes/usage.ts:44-52` (`validateLicenseAndGetCredits`) does **not**: it computes `isValid = data.valid === true` after `response.json().catch(() => ({}))` and unconditionally calls `cacheLicense` — so a 500, an HTML error page, or an empty body all poison the cache with `isValid:false`. Verify every `cacheLicense` call is preceded by a status check.\n\n### I4: Every STT adapter that returns a non-empty transcript returns a strictly positive `durationSeconds` (falling back to `estimateSecondsFromBytes` when the upstream duration field is absent or zero).\nWhy it matters: `costUsd` is `durationSeconds × rate` for duration-billed providers, so a missing field means a real, provider-billed transcription is charged **0 credits** — a free-transcription oracle triggered by any upstream response-shape change.\nSearch strategy: `grep -ln \"estimateSecondsFromBytes\" hyperwhisper-cloud/src/providers/*.ts` and diff against the 11 STT adapters. Present in `assemblyai.ts`, `mistral.ts`, `soniox.ts`, `openai.ts`, and `google-chirp.ts` (via its own `estimateAudioSeconds`); **absent in `deepgram.ts`, `groq.ts`, `elevenlabs.ts`, `xai-stt.ts`, `azure-mai.ts`**. For each, read where `durationSeconds` is derived (`data.metadata.duration`, `data.duration`, last word `.end`, `data.durationMilliseconds`) and confirm a guard before `compute*TranscriptionCost(...)`.\n\n### I5: Every Stripe webhook handler that mutates licenses or credits dedupes on a stable Stripe id before mutating, and returns non-2xx (so Stripe retries) on any *transient* failure.\nWhy it matters: Stripe delivers at-least-once and fires both `checkout.session.completed` and `checkout.session.async_payment_succeeded` for the same purchase; a missing guard double-grants credits, and swallowing an error returns 200 and permanently loses the grant.\nSearch strategy: in `nextjs/lib/services/stripe-webhook.ts`, grep `grantCreditLot(\\|grantCreditsForStripeEvent(\\|refundCreditGrant(\\|updateAccountKey(`. `handleCreditPurchase` correctly routes through `grantCreditsForStripeEvent` (keyed on `stripe_processed_events.stripe_object_id`); `handleLicensePurchase:126` calls the **un-deduped `grantCreditLot({sourceType:\"license_bundle\", sourceId: session.id})`** and its only guard is a prior `findAccountByStripeSession` read — check whether `grantCreditLotInTransaction`'s `onConflictDoNothing` target actually covers `(sourceType, sourceId)`. Also `app/api/webhooks/stripe/route.ts:121-130`: `handleChargeRefunded` errors are caught and the route still returns `{received:true}` — a refund clawback lost to a transient DB blip is never retried.\n\n### I6: No credit amount, price, or quantity that determines what a user is charged or granted is taken from client-controlled input without server-side re-derivation or a hard bound.\nWhy it matters: this is the direct path to minting free credits or draining another key's wallet.\nSearch strategy: `grep -rn \"body.amount\\|input.amount\\|metadata.credit_amount\\|creditAmount\" nextjs/app/api nextjs/lib nextjs/server`. `POST /api/license/credits` accepts `amount` straight off the wire (bounded only by `validateCreditDeductionAmount`: finite, >0, ≤1,000,000, 2 dp) and deducts it — with the license key as the sole credential and no rate limit, so anyone holding a leaked key can zero a balance in one request. `session.metadata.credit_amount` is trusted in `handleCreditPurchase`; confirm nothing but `/api/checkout/credits` can write that metadata. Confirm `computeCreditPurchase()` is the only source of `creditCents`/`feeCents` and that `components/credits/CreditsPurchase.tsx:92` sends only `amount`, never a price.\n\n### I7: Every unauthenticated Next.js route handler whose work fans out to a paid third party or writes a durable object is rate-limited.\nWhy it matters: one cheap POST maps 1:1 to a Polar call, a Stripe customer/session creation, or a 6-provider model fan-out — free amplification and quota/cost burn against an open endpoint.\nSearch strategy: `grep -rLn \"RateLimiter\\|\\.limit(\" nextjs/app/api/**/route.ts` and compare against `nextjs/lib/rate-limit.ts` (only `downloadEmailRateLimiter` and `licenseValidateRateLimiter` exist). `/api/license/validate` and `/api/license/activate` are covered; `/api/license/credits` (GET **and** the deducting POST), `/api/checkout/credits` (creates real `stripe.customers` + Checkout Sessions), `/api/download` (outbound appcast fetch + dynamic RegExp), and `/api/internal/models` are not. For `/api/internal/models` and `/models`, verify the `s-maxage=3600` header plus `fetchAvailableModels`'s in-memory TTL actually bound the fan-out on a cache-busted or cold-lambda request.\n\n### I8: Any handler returning or mutating a specific user's data derives that user from the Better Auth session (or an admin check), never from a client-supplied id; middleware cookie-presence is never the only gate.\nWhy it matters: `middleware.ts` checks only that `better-auth.session_token` **exists** for `/{locale}/user/*` — a forged or expired cookie value passes it. If a page or procedure then trusts that, an unauthenticated caller reads customer data.\nSearch strategy: for each of `app/[locale]/user/(authenticated)/{dashboard,devices,customers}/page.tsx` and `layout.tsx`, grep for `auth.api.getSession`; any page that renders account data without one relies on the presence-only check. Cross-check the tRPC routers: `grep -n \"publicProcedure\\|protectedProcedure\\|adminProcedure\" nextjs/server/api/routers/**/*.ts` — every `admin/*` procedure must use `adminProcedure` (`ctx.isAdmin` from `user.role === \"admin\"`), and every `customer.*` procedure must key off `ctx.user.id`/`ctx.user.email`, never an `input`-supplied id. Also verify `src/db/schema/auth.ts` marks `role` as `input: false` so it cannot be set at sign-up.\n\n### I9: Every comparison of a caller-supplied credential against an env secret is timing-safe **and** fails closed when the env var is unset or empty.\nWhy it matters: `timingSafeEqualSecret(received, expected)` returns `false` when `expected` is falsy — good — but any `===` comparison, or a route that skips the check when the env var is missing, silently opens an admin/mint endpoint in a misconfigured environment.\nSearch strategy: `grep -rn \"process.env\\..*SECRET\\|process.env\\..*TOKEN\\|process.env\\..*KEY\" nextjs/app/api nextjs/lib | grep -n \"===\\|!==\\|==\"`. Confirm all three `/api/internal/*` routes call `timingSafeEqualSecret(secret, process.env.HYPERWHISPER_INTERNAL_SECRET)` before any DB work (they do at line ~11 of each). In `app/api/webhooks/add-blog-post/route.ts`, check the interaction between `OUTRANK_WEBHOOK_SIGNING_SECRET` and `OUTRANK_WEBHOOK_TOKEN`: when signing is enabled, verify the bearer path cannot be used as an alternative bypass, and that a missing `OUTRANK_WEBHOOK_TOKEN` doesn't make `timingSafeEqualSecret` the only barrier to a mint-shaped mutation. Also `app/api/webhooks/stripe/route.ts:33-40` correctly 500s on a missing `STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET` — confirm no sibling handler does the opposite.\n\n### I10: Every `fetch()` to a third-party API carries an `AbortSignal.timeout` or an `AbortController`.\nWhy it matters: a hung upstream leaves an unresolved promise pinning the request scope — on a 1 GB Fly machine that bloats the Bun worker until OOM-kill, and on Vercel it burns the full function max-duration.\nSearch strategy: `grep -rn \"fetch(\" hyperwhisper-cloud/src nextjs/lib nextjs/app | grep -v \"signal:\"`. In the cloud service all 10 STT adapters route through `fetchWithTimeout()` in `providers/utils.ts` (default `DEFAULT_PROVIDER_TIMEOUT_MS = 15_000`), but **all 7 LLM provider files — `anthropic.ts`, `cerebras.ts`, `groq-llm.ts`, `gemini-llm.ts`, `mistral-llm.ts`, `openai-llm.ts`, `xai-llm.ts` — use bare `fetch()` with no timeout** (`streamAnthropicChat`'s AbortController fires only on client disconnect, not on a stall). Note `gcs-storage.ts` maintains a *second, duplicated* `fetchWithTimeoutMs` implementation that can drift from `providers/utils.ts`. On the Next.js side check `lib/services/model-list.ts` `apiGet()` and the Polar SDK calls in `src/lib/license-validation.ts` (no timeout is configurable there, yet it sits behind an unauthenticated endpoint).\n\n### I11: In the WebSocket bridge, every acquired resource is released on every path, and closing one side always closes the other.\nWhy it matters: an orphaned upstream Deepgram socket keeps metering billable audio and holds a Fly file descriptor; a leaked `setInterval` survives the request.\nSearch strategy: read `hyperwhisper-cloud/src/routes/ws-streaming-deepgram.ts` end to end and check each early `return`. The `MAX_SESSION_AUDIO_BYTES` breach at line 308 sends an error and calls `clientSocket.close(1009)` but **never calls `closeUpstream()` / `deepgramWs.close()`** — verify whether the resulting client close reliably fires `onClose`. Also: `pingInterval` is cleared only inside `endSession()`; the `apiKey`-missing early return at line 238 sets it never, but confirm no path sets the interval then exits without `endSession()`. Grep `setInterval(\\|clearInterval(\\|getReader()\\|releaseLock()\\|\\.cancel(` across `src/` — `providers/anthropic.ts`'s `streamAnthropicChat` `start()` catch block never calls `reader.cancel()` when the failure is not a client abort.\n\n### I12: The provider and model reported back to the client (`X-STT-Provider`, `X-STT-Model`, deduction `metadata`) always names the provider/model that actually ran and was priced.\nWhy it matters: `transcribe.ts:669` does `result.source as Provider` and looks the price up from that; if an adapter returns a `source` that isn't its own registry id, or reports a `model` it didn't run, the charge is computed against the wrong rate table and support has no audit trail.\nSearch strategy: `grep -n \"source:\" hyperwhisper-cloud/src/providers/*.ts` and check each returned literal against `TranscriptionSource` in `providers/types.ts` **and** against `SttProviderId` in `lib/stt-models.ts` — these are two parallel unions that must stay in sync (`TranscriptionSource` additionally carries `'no_speech'`). Then confirm `usedModel = result.model || attemptModel` (line 511) is what reaches both the `X-STT-Model` header and `stt_model` in the `deductCredits` metadata, and that `getProviderDef(current).defaultModel` on a fallback hop matches the rate actually used in `estimateCreditsForProviderFallbacks`. Also check `cost-calculator.ts`'s `rates[model] ?? rates[defaultModel]` fallback — an unknown model id is silently re-priced at the default rate.\n\n### I13: Every value crossing a trust boundary is validated (or allow-listed/escaped) before it is used to index, interpolate, allocate, or render.\nWhy it matters: these are the crash-and-injection sinks — an unvalidated `req.json()` destructure 500s, an unescaped name lands script in a transactional email from a trusted sender, an unbounded `Content-Length` allocates memory before auth.\nSearch strategy: (a) `grep -rn \"await req.json()\\|await request.json()\\|c.req.json()\" nextjs/app/api hyperwhisper-cloud/src` and confirm each result is shape-checked before destructuring — `app/api/license/credits/route.ts:82` does `const body = await req.json(); const { license_key, amount, metadata } = body;` with no null guard and an entirely unvalidated `metadata` that is then logged. (b) `grep -rn \"escapeHtml\" nextjs/lib/templates/` — all four templates escape `customerName`; verify every *other* interpolated field in the 17/15/21/14 `${...}` sites is either a number or system-generated. (c) `grep -rn \"dangerouslySetInnerHTML\" nextjs` — `app/[locale]/blog/[slug]/page.tsx:170` renders `post.html` raw from the Outrank webhook. (d) `grep -rn \"new RegExp(\" nextjs` — `app/api/download/route.ts:66` builds a RegExp from `arch`, guarded by `parseWindowsArch`'s allow-list; confirm no sibling does the same without one. (e) Confirm `Content-Length` is parsed and bounded *before* `arrayBuffer()`/`formData()` on every cloud route.\n\n### I14: No user-facing path hardcodes a single locale, and every message key referenced by a component exists in all 40 locale files.\nWhy it matters: `localePrefix: \"always\"` over 40 locales means a hardcoded `/en/...` silently strips 39 languages from a cache invalidation or a post-auth redirect, and a missing key makes `next-intl` throw at render — a hard 500 on that locale only, invisible from an English-language smoke test.\nSearch strategy: `grep -rn '\"/en/\\|`/en/\\|/en/' nextjs/app nextjs/src nextjs/lib` — `app/api/webhooks/add-blog-post/route.ts:386,403` calls `revalidatePath(\"/en/blog/...\")` and `revalidatePath(\"/en/blog\")` only, so 39 locales keep serving stale blog indexes; `src/lib/license-key-redirect.ts:1` pins `DEFAULT_LICENSE_KEY_REDIRECT = \"/en/user/dashboard\"`. Then diff key sets: `for f in nextjs/messages/*.json; do echo \"$f $(python3 -c 'import json,sys;d=json.load(open(sys.argv[1]));print(len(json.dumps(d)))' $f)\"; done` — better, flatten and set-diff each of the 40 files against `en.json`. Finally, `grep -rLn \"setRequestLocale\" nextjs/app/\\[locale\\]/**/page.tsx` — only `app/[locale]/layout.tsx` calls it today, so verify against `routing.ts` whether any statically-rendered page under `[locale]` needs it to avoid bailing to dynamic rendering or leaking the wrong locale.\n\n## Partition\n\n*(verified programmatically: all 210 files in `bug-hunt/scope-files.txt` land in exactly one
